You\'ve read this rule and nothing else. Where will the market be on Saturday?\\", options: [\'In the town hall.\', \'Outdoors, as usual.\', \\"The rule alone doesn\'t settle it.\\"], correct: 2, feedback: [\'You treated the rule as a weather report. It tells you what follows if it rains. Nobody has told you that it will rain.\', \\"You assumed it won\'t rain. The rule hasn\'t told you that either, so the market\'s location is still open.\\", \\"The rule describes what would happen if it rains. It\'s a promise about a possible case, not a report that the case happened.\\"] },\\r\\n    { prompt: \\"Saturday arrives, and it\'s raining. Now where is the market?\\", options: [\'In the town hall.\', \'Still not settled.\'], correct: 0, feedback: [\'Now you have a fact, not just a rule. Rain on Saturday meets the condition, so the rule puts the market in the town hall.\', \\"The fact changes things. The rule covers a rainy Saturday, and that\'s what we have, so the market\'s location is settled.\\"] },\\r\\n  ] },\\r\\n  { rule: \'Every student going on the museum trip has a signed permission slip.\', questions: [\\r\\n    { prompt: \'Which description matches this rule?\', options: [\'Going on the trip is enough to know a student has a signed slip.\', \'Having a signed slip is enough to know a student is going on the trip.\', \'Each one is enough to know the other.\'], correct: 0, feedback: [\'Pick any student on the trip: the rule says they have a signed slip. }}\n+            onFocus={() => setSelected(index)} onClick={() => setSelected(index)}>\n+            <span className=\"ni-number\" aria-hidden=\"true\">{String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')}</span>\n+            <span>{expression.toLowerCase()}</span><span className=\"ni-indicator\" aria-hidden=\"true\">↗</span>\n+          </button>\n+        </li>)}\n+      </ol>\n+      <div className=\"ni-examples\">\n+        {ordered.map((example, index) => <section key={expressionOrder[index]} id={`${id}-example-${index}`}\n+          aria-label={`${expressionOrder[index]} example`} hidden={selected !== index} className=\"ni-example\">\n+          <p className=\"ni-example-label\">{String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')} / {String(expressionOrder.length).padStart(2, '0')} · The sentence and its meaning</p>\n+          <dl className=\"nc-indicator-examples\">{example}</dl>\n+          <div className=\"ni-requirement\"><span>Necessary condition</span><strong>{index === 3 ? 'A tutor is present where the seminar takes place.' : 'You are a student.'}</strong></div>\n+        </section>)}\n+      </div>\n+    </div>\n+  </div>;\n+}\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-conditional-bootcamp/src/bootcamps/conditionals/components/NecessaryIndicatorExplorer.test.tsx\n+import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, within } from '@testing-library/react';\n+import { afterEach, expect, it } from 'vitest';\n+import { conditionalReferenceContent } from './ConditionalReferenceSequence';\n+\n+afterEach(cleanup);\n+\n+it('keeps indicator order and complete examples together across pointer, keyboard and touch selection', () => {\n+  const view = render(conditionalReferenceContent());\n+  const lesson = within(view.getByRole('region', { name: 'Group 2 indicator words' }));\n+  const list = lesson.getByRole('list', { name: 'Necessary-condition indicators' });\n+  const buttons = within(list).getAllByRole('button');\n+  expect(buttons.map(button => button.textContent?.replace(/^\\d+/, '').replace('↗', ''))).toEqual([\n+    'only', 'only if', 'only when', 'only where', 'always', 'requires', 'must',\n+  ]);\n+  for (const button of buttons) {\n+    fireEvent.focus(button);\n+    expect(button).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');\n+    expect(document.getElementById(button.getAttribute('aria-controls')!)).toBeVisible();\n+    expect(lesson.getAllByRole('region', { name: /example$/ })).toHaveLength(1);\n+  }\n+  fireEvent.click(buttons[3]);\n+  const place = lesson.getByRole('region', { name: 'Only where example' });\n+  expect(place).toHaveTextContent('A seminar takes place only where a tutor is present.');\n+  expect(place).toHaveTextContent('A tutor could also be somewhere without a seminar.');\n+  fireEvent.click(buttons[4]);\n+  expect(lesson.getByRole('region', { name: 'Always example' })).toHaveTextContent('after enrollment ends');\n+  fireEvent.click(buttons[6]);\n+  expect(lesson.getByRole('region', { name: 'Must example' })).toHaveTextContent('not a task someone has been told to complete');\n+});\n*** End Patch"));

ThemeContext.tsx lets users save \'dark\', and the production route renders `cb-shell oc-workspace` (routes.tsx:209). The necessary page has a light-background override for sources 2 and 4 (sufficient-condition-experience.css:14), but the Must arrow (data-reference-source=8) does not. Result in dark theme: the \'Read left to right…\' instruction and the \'Sufficient condition\' / \'Necessary condition\' role labels render at about L0.94 on an L0.95–0.99 card, so they are effectively invisible, and the arrow is L0.80 teal on near-white. In the other direction, `.ma-lesson { --co-muted: oklch(46%) }` (line 1) is fixed, so muted text outside the card (e.g. `.ma-compact-note`) sits at L46% on dark paper (about L20%), which is too low-contrast. must-arrow-review.md records an earlier fix that replaced light-only literals with light/dark tokens; this redesign brings the problem back. At ≤620px (necessary-indicator-explorer.css:30-37) the panel sits below the list (one column, or a 2-column grid of buttons at 381–620px). A mouse user in a docked browser who clicks \'only if\' and moves down to read the panel passes over the buttons below it (\'only when\', \'only where\', … \'must\'). The panel ends up showing whichever item the pointer crossed last, not the one they clicked. The teaching preferences name docked width as an inspection target. Also, `button:hover { transform: translateX(3px) }` is not gated by `(hover: hover)`, so on touch devices it sticks on the last tapped button.\\",\\r\\n        \\"fix\\": \\"Separate preview from commit. text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"@'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport re\nroot=Path('src/bootcamps/conditionals/components')\np=root/'ConditionalReferenceSequence.tsx'\ns=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')\nstart=s.index('          <NecessaryIndicatorExplorer>')\nend=s.index('          </NecessaryIndicatorExplorer>',start)+len('          </NecessaryIndicatorExplorer>')\nentries=dict(re.findall(r'<div><dt>(.*?)</dt><dd>(.*?)</dd></div>',s[start:end]))\norder=['Only','Only if','Only when','Only where','Always','Requires','Must']\nassert set(entries)==set(order)\ndata=[]\nfor name in order:\n    necessary='A tutor is present where the seminar takes place.' if name=='Only where' else 'You are a student.'\n    data.append('  { expression: '+repr(name)+', necessaryCondition: '+repr(necessary)+', content: <>'+entries[name]+'</> },')\np.write_text(s[:start]+'          <NecessaryIndicatorExplorer/>'+s[end:],encoding='utf-8')\ncomponent=\"\"\"import { useId, useRef, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from 'react';\nimport './necessary-indicator-explorer.css';\n\nconst examples = [\n\"\"\"+'\\n'.join(data)+\"\"\"\n];\n\nexport function NecessaryIndicatorExplorer() {\n  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(0);\n  const tabs = useRef<(HTMLButtonElement | null)[]>([]);\n  const id = useId();\n  function navigate(event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLButtonElement>, index: number) {\n    let next: number;\n    switch (event.key) {\n      case 'ArrowDown': case 'ArrowRight': next = (index + 1) % examples.length; break;\n      case 'ArrowUp': case 'ArrowLeft': next = (index + examples.length - 1) % examples.length; break;\n      case 'Home': next = 0; break;\n      case 'End': next = examples.length - 1; break;\n      default: return;\n    }\n    event.preventDefault();\n    setSelected(next);\n    tabs.current[next]?.focus();\n  }\n  return <div className=\"ni-explorer\">\n    <p className=\"ni-instruction\">Choose an expression to see the necessary condition it identifies in a complete sentence.</p>\n    <div className=\"ni-layout\">\n      <ol className=\"ni-expression-list\" role=\"tablist\" aria-label=\"Necessary-condition indicators\" aria-orientation=\"vertical\">\n        {examples.map((example, index) => <li key={example.expression} role=\"presentation\">\n          <button type=\"button\" role=\"tab\" id={`${id}-tab-${index}`} aria-selected={selected === index}\n            tabIndex={selected === index ? 0 : -1} aria-controls={`${id}-example-${index}`}\n            ref={element => { tabs.current[index] = element; }} onKeyDown={event => navigate(event, index)}\n            onPointerEnter={event => {\n              if (event.pointerType === 'mouse' && window.matchMedia('(hover: hover) and (min-width: 621px)').matches) setSelected(index);\n            }}\n            onFocus={() => setSelected(index)} onClick={() => setSelected(index)}>\n            <span className=\"ni-number\" aria-hidden=\"true\">{String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')}</span>\n            <span>{example.expression.toLowerCase()}</span><span className=\"ni-indicator\" aria-hidden=\"true\">↗</span>\n          </button>\n        </li>)}\n      </ol>\n      <div className=\"ni-examples\">\n        {examples.map((example, index) => <section key={example.expression} id={`${id}-example-${index}`}\n          role=\"tabpanel\" aria-labelledby={`${id}-tab-${index}`} tabIndex={0} hidden={selected !== index} className=\"ni-example\">\n          <p className=\"ni-example-label\">{String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')} / {String(examples.length).padStart(2, '0')} · The sentence and its meaning</p>\n          <dl className=\"nc-indicator-examples\"><div><dt>{example.expression}</dt><dd>{example.content}</dd></div></dl>\n          <div className=\"ni-requirement\"><span>Necessary condition</span><strong>{example.necessaryCondition}</strong></div>\n        </section>)}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </div>;\n}\n\"\"\"\n(root/'NecessaryIndicatorExplorer.tsx').write_text(component,encoding='utf-8')\np=root/'necessary-indicator-explorer.css'\ns=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8').replace('aria-pressed','aria-selected')\ncase_rules='\\n'.join(line for line in s.splitlines() if 'nc-case-rule' in line)\ns='\\n'.join(line for line in s.splitlines() if 'nc-case-rule' not in line)+'\\n'\ns=s.replace('#root .ni-expression-list button:hover { transform: translateX(3px); }','@media (hover: hover) { #root .ni-expression-list button:hover { transform: translateX(3px); } }')\np.write_text(s,encoding='utf-8')\np=root/'necessary-condition-tabs.css'\np.write_text(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')+'\\n'+case_rules+'\\n',encoding='utf-8')\np=root/'must-arrow-note.css'\ns=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8').replace('#root .ma-lesson { --co-muted: oklch(46% .03 252); }','#root .ma-example { --co-ink: oklch(25% .04 252); --co-teal: oklch(43% .08 180); --co-polish-line: oklch(82% .02 82); --ma-muted: oklch(46% .03 252); color: var(--co-ink); }').replace('var(--co-muted)','var(--ma-muted)')\ns=s.replace('color: var(--ma-muted); margin-top: 1rem;', 'color: var(--co-polish-muted); margin-top: 1rem;')\np.write_text(s,encoding='utf-8')\n'@ | python -",workdir:"C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-conditional-bootcamp",max_output_tokens:1000}));

text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"@'\nimport { cleanup, fireEvent, render, within } from '@testing-library/react';\nimport { afterEach, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\nimport { NecessaryIndicatorExplorer } from './NecessaryIndicatorExplorer';\n\nafterEach(() => { cleanup(); vi.unstubAllGlobals(); });\n\nfunction pointAt(element: HTMLElement, pointerType: string) {\n  const event = new Event('pointerover', { bubbles: true });\n  Object.defineProperty(event, 'pointerType', { value: pointerType });\n  fireEvent(element, event);\n}\n\nit('keeps one ordered complete example and its exact necessary condition available for each tab', () => {\n  const view = render(<NecessaryIndicatorExplorer/>);\n  const tabs = view.getAllByRole('tab');\n  expect(tabs.map(tab => tab.textContent?.replace(/^\\d+/, '').replace('↗', ''))).toEqual([\n    'only', 'only if', 'only when', 'only where', 'always', 'requires', 'must',\n  ]);\n  for (const tab of tabs) {\n    fireEvent.click(tab);\n    expect(tab).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');\n    const panel = view.getByRole('tabpanel');\n    expect(panel).toHaveAttribute('aria-labelledby', tab.id);\n    expect(panel.id).toBe(tab.getAttribute('aria-controls'));\n    expect(panel.querySelector('blockquote')).toBeVisible();\n    const requirement = panel.querySelector('.ni-requirement strong');\n    expect(requirement).toHaveTextContent(tab.textContent?.includes('only where')\n      ? 'A tutor is present where the seminar takes place.' : 'You are a student.');\n  }\n  fireEvent.click(view.getByRole('tab', { name: 'only where' }));\n  expect(view.getByRole('tabpanel')).toHaveTextContent('A tutor could also be somewhere without a seminar.');\n  fireEvent.click(view.getByRole('tab', { name: 'always' }));\n  expect(view.getByRole('tabpanel')).toHaveTextContent('after enrollment ends');\n  fireEvent.click(view.getByRole('tab', { name: 'must' }));\n  expect(view.getByRole('tabpanel')).toHaveTextContent('not a task someone has been told to complete');\n});\n\nit('supports roving keyboard focus, arrow wrapping, Home and End', () => {\n  const view = render(<NecessaryIndicatorExplorer/>);\n  const tabs = within(view.getByRole('tablist')).getAllByRole('tab');\n  tabs[0].focus();\n  fireEvent.keyDown(tabs[0], { key: 'ArrowDown' });\n  expect(tabs[1]).toHaveFocus();\n  expect(tabs[1]).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');\n  expect(tabs[0]).toHaveAttribute('tabindex', '-1');\n  fireEvent.keyDown(tabs[1], { key: 'End' });\n  expect(tabs[6]).toHaveFocus();\n  fireEvent.keyDown(tabs[6], { key: 'ArrowRight' });\n  expect(tabs[0]).toHaveFocus();\n  fireEvent.keyDown(tabs[0], { key: 'ArrowUp' });\n  expect(tabs[6]).toHaveFocus();\n  fireEvent.keyDown(tabs[6], { key: 'Home' });\n  expect(tabs[0]).toHaveFocus();\n  expect(tabs.filter(tab => tab.tabIndex === 0)).toHaveLength(1);\n});\n\nit('supports mouse hover only beside the panel, ignoring touch and preserving a clicked narrow-layout choice', () => {\n  const media = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ matches: true });\n  vi.stubGlobal('matchMedia', media);\n  const view = render(<NecessaryIndicatorExplorer/>);\n  const tabs = view.getAllByRole('tab');\n  pointAt(tabs[1], 'mouse');\n  expect(tabs[1]).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');\n  expect(media).toHaveBeenCalledWith('(hover: hover) and (min-width: 621px)');\n  pointAt(tabs[3], 'touch');\n  expect(tabs[1]).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');\n  media.mockReturnValue({ matches: false });\n  fireEvent.click(tabs[2]);\n  pointAt(tabs[5], 'mouse');\n  expect(tabs[2]).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');\n  fireEvent.click(tabs[3]);\n
